54:["$","p",null,{"children":["The TCP client pings the server on its own every ",["$","code",null,{"children":"HeartbeatInterval"}]," while connected, so an idle session survives the server's heartbeat verification and consumer-group memberships and assignments stay fresh. The ping goes through the regular request path: a lost session is repaired by the same reconnect and auto-login any other request would trigger. Heartbeat failures are logged and the loop keeps going until the client is disposed."]}]
